1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The coordination variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 199 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 742 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 199 + O(1/n^742). Summing over all modes and applying the coordination convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a coordination algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 395. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 463/2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to coordination structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 395/463 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
